  => err=False /**
      * "telelean: Initialize Model Here" (D-28, docs/m1-plan.md sections 3 and 7.2), without the vscode API:
      *
      *   1. inspect the folder (runner `inspectFolder`): empty, already a model, a dependency-free Lake project, or
      *      unsupported (with the reason, e.g. Lake dependencies such as Mathlib, which M1 refuses);
      *   2. empty folder: ask the first subject's name (validated; suggested from the folder name);
      *      Lake project: confirm adding `.telelean/` and `.gitignore` lines;
      *   3. plan the files (runner `planInitialize`) and write them through the EditApplier;
      *   4. when the folder is not inside a git repository, offer `git init` for telelean's history (modal);
      *   5. open the board.
      */
     import * as path from 'node:path';
     
     import { isSubjectName, SUBJECT_NAME_PATTERN, type FileChange, type HistoryAvailability } from '@telelean/core';
     import type { FolderInspection, InitializeOptions } from '@telelean/runner/workspace/index.js';
     
     /**
      * The suggested first subject for a folder: its name as a capitalised identifier (`mental_model` -> `MentalModel`,
      * `spyro notes` -> `SpyroNotes`); characters outside `[A-Za-z0-9]` separate words and are dropped; `Subject` when
      * nothing usable is left (or the result would start with a digit).

     /**
      * What a computed vocabulary item is (D-3 simple shape check, docs/m1-plan.md section 6.4):
      * `sort` (`axiom T : Type`), `predicate` (`... -> Prop`, arity >= 0), `constant` (`opaque c : T`),
      * `function` (a data-valued function symbol), `presupposition` (`axiom T.exists : Nonempty T`),
      * `definition` (a def/abbrev/structure/inductive/instance without a block: needs endorsement, D-4),
      * `lemma` (a theorem without a block: proof-only, conservative), `unmarked-axiom` (a Prop-valued axiom without a
      * block: a hidden assumption, TL104), `other`.
      */
     export type VocabKind =
       | 'sort'
       | 'predicate'
       | 'constant'
       | 'function'
       | 'presupposition'
       | 'definition'
       | 'lemma'
       | 'unmarked-axiom'
       | 'other';
     
     export type VocabFlag =
       /** TL104: a Prop-valued axiom with no block. */
       | 'unmarked-assumption'
       /** TL111: violates the simple vocabulary shape rule. */
       | 'shape-violation'
       /** D-4: a definition on a statement path needs the user's endorsement. */
       | 'definitional-choice'
       /** Facts for its file are stale. */
       | 'stale'
       /** Added by a proposal (M2 "+3 new"). */
       | 'new';

     /** What follows a block: the declaration it binds to by adjacency, or one after a blank line. */
     function findFollowingDecl(text: string, end: number, scan: DeclScan, blockComments: ReadonlySet<LeanComment>): { adjacent?: number; gap?: number } {
       const { items, decls } = scan;
       let p = end;
       for (;;) {
         let q = p;
         let newlines = 0;
         for (; q < text.length; q++) {
           const c = text.charCodeAt(q);
           if (c === 0x0a) newlines++;
           else if (c !== 0x20 && c !== 0x09 && c !== 0x0d) break;
         }
         if (q >= text.length) return {};
         const blank = newlines >= 2;
         const item: Item | undefined = items[lowerBound(items.length, (i) => (items[i]?.start ?? Infinity) < q)];
         if (item === undefined || item.start !== q) return {};
         if (item.comment !== undefined && blockComments.has(item.comment)) return {};
         if (item.comment !== undefined && (item.comment.kind === 'line' || item.comment.kind === 'block')) {
           if (blank) return {};
           p = item.end;
           continue;
         }
         // A doc comment or a token: is it inside a declaration's header (between its start and its keyword)?
         const index = lowerBound(decls.length, (i) => (decls[i]?.span.start ?? Infinity) <= q) - 1;
         const decl = decls[index];
         if (decl === undefined || q > decl.keywordSpan.start) return {};
         return blank ? { gap: ind

     const LOGICAL = /(^|[\s(])(∀|∃|¬|∧|∨|↔|=|≠|True|False)($|[\s)])/;
     
     /** Syntactic guess of `typeSort` when Lean's is unavailable. */
     function inferTypeSort(shape: DeclShape, sorts: ReadonlySet<string>, propHeads: ReadonlySet<string>): string | undefined {
       const type = shape.type;
       if (type === undefined) return undefined;
       if (type === 'Type') return 'Type 1';
       if (/^(Type|Sort)\b/.test(type)) return undefined;
       const last = shape.parts[shape.parts.length - 1] ?? '';
       if (last === 'Prop') return 'Type';
       if (shape.parts.every((p) => sorts.has(p))) return 'Type';
       if (/^Nonempty\b/.test(type)) return 'Prop';
       if (LOGICAL.test(type)) return 'Prop';
       const head = /^[^\s()]+/.exec(last)?.[0];
       if (head && propHeads.has(head)) return 'Prop';
       return undefined;
     }

         return last.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + last.slice(1);
       });

  => err=False /**
      * Text helpers for the semantic layer: line indexes, Lean (code point) to LSP (UTF-16) positions, and the few
      * pieces of Lean source scanning the facts need (docstrings, the `instance` keyword, import headers).
      *
      * Lean reports message positions as a 1-based line and a 0-based column counted in Unicode code points; the board
      * (and the `.ilean` files) use LSP positions: 0-based line and UTF-16 code units. A character outside the Basic
      * Multilingual Plane (e.g. `𝔸`) is one code point but two UTF-16 units, so columns after it differ.
      */
     import type { Position } from '@telelean/core';
     
     /** Start offsets (UTF-16 indexes into the string) of every line; lines end at `\n` (a preceding `\r` stays in the line). */
     export function lineStarts(text: string): number[] {
       const starts = [0];
       for (let i = text.indexOf('\n'); i >= 0; i = text.indexOf('\n', i + 1)) starts.push(i + 1);
       return starts;
     }
     
     /** The text of a 0-based line (without its `\n`), or undefined past the end. */
     export function lineText(text: string, starts: readonly number[], line: number): string | undefined {
       const start = starts[line];
       if (start === undefined) return undefined;
       const next = starts[line + 1];
       return text.slice(start, next === undefined ? text.length : next - 1);
     }
     
     /**
      * UTF-16 length of the first `codePoints` code points of `line`. Columns past the end of the line count one unit
      * each (Lean can point just past the last character).
      */
     export function codePointsToUtf16(line:

       /** Lean's full range (fresh facts) of each declaration an op removes or replaces must lie inside its syntactic span. */
       private checkSpansAgainstLean(path: string, ops: readonly LeanOp[]): void {
         const state = this.state(path);
         if (state.batches === undefined || state.batches.length > 0 || !this.model.freshFiles.has(path)) return;
         const parsed = this.parsed(path);
         let map: LineMap | undefined;
         for (const op of ops) {
           const name = op.op === 'remove-decl' ? op.name : op.op === 'remove-holds' || op.op === 'holds-theorem-to-axiom' ? holdsName(op.statement) : undefined;

           const facts = this.model.declFacts.get(name);
           if (!decl || !facts || facts.file !== path) continue;
           map ??= new LineMap(parsed.text);

           if (leanEnd > decl.span.end) {

               'unsafe',

             );
           }
         }
       }

     function DefinitionsStep({ proposal, review }: { proposal: ProposalView; review: ReviewUi }) {

     function ResultStep({ proposal, review }: { proposal: ProposalView; review: ReviewUi }) {
       const actions = useBoardActions();

         return (
           <div className="tl-review__hidden" data-testid="result-hidden">

             <button type="button" className="tl-button tl-button--secondary" onClick={() => actions.revealResult()}>
               Show result
             </button>
           </div>
         );
       }
       return <ReportCard proposal={proposal} />;
     }

     /** The DescribeContext the RunManager builds from the current board (the user's real files). */
     function contextOf(graph: TlGraph, selected: readonly string[]): DescribeContext {
       const owner = new Map<string, string>();
       for (const node of graph.nodes) {
         if (node.lean.statement === undefined) continue;
         owner.set(node.lean.statement, node.id);
         owner.set(holdsName(node.lean.statement), node.id);
       }
       return { sentenceOf: (id) => graph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id)?.naturalName, nodeOfDecl: (name) => owner.get(name), selected };
     }

  => err=False   // ---- RunManager: feedback (docs/m2-plan.md section 4.8)
     
       async feedback(runId: string, text: string): Promise<ActionOutcome> {
         return this.exclusive('sending feedback', () => this.feedbackNow(runId, text));
       }
     
       private async feedbackNow(runId: string, text: string): Promise<ActionOutcome> {
         const run = this.runFor(runId);
         if (run === undefined) return refused('not-found', 'That run is not active any more.');
         if (run.view.state !== 'review') return refused('conflict', 'Feedback is possible once Claude has finished its round.');
         const trimmed = text.trim();
         if (trimmed === '') return refused('invalid', 'Write what Claude should change.');
         await this.resolveAttempt(run, 'superseded');
         const head = await headCommit(run.shadow);
         this.startNewRound(run, 'formalise');
         if (head !== undefined) run.p.roundStartCommit = head.id;
         this.notice(run, 'info', 'info', `Round ${run.view.round}: your feedback was sent to Claude.`);
         this.activity(run, 'system', `Round ${run.view.round} (Feedback): “${sentenceShort(trimmed, 120)}”`);
         this.setState(run, 'working');
         await this.save(run, { run: true, proposal: true });
         const outcome = await this.continueSession(run, feedbackTurn(trimmed, this.o.settings.caps));
         return outcome.ok ? okOutcome('Feedback sent to Claude') : outcome;
       }

     /**
      * A deletion of `span` that keeps the spacing tidy. When the span covers whole lines it takes their line break
      * too, plus one blank line when that would otherwise leave two blank lines in a row (or a blank line at the start
      * of the file); when it removes the end of the file, the blank line before it goes. A span sharing its line with
      * other code removes only itself and the adjacent spaces.
      */
     export function deletionEdit(text: string, span: Span, lines: Lines = new Lines(text)): TextEdit {
       const startLine = lines.lineOf(span.start);

       async baseDrift(shadow: ShadowWorkspace, readReal: ReadRealText, realFiles?: readonly string[]): Promise<string[]> {
         const base = shadowFilesOf(await shadow.baseFiles(), shadow.library);
         const paths = new Set([...base.keys(), ...(realFiles ?? []).filter((rel) => isModelOrProjectFile(rel, shadow.library))]);
         const out: string[] = [];
         for (const rel of [...paths].sort()) if ((await readReal(rel)) !== (base.get(rel) ?? null)) out.push(rel);
         return out;
       }

  |   if (shown === base) return { ok: true, text: real };
  |   if (real === base || real === shown) return { ok: true, text: shown };
  |   if (base === null || shown === null || real === null) return { ok: false };
  |   const patch = structuredPatch(path, path, base, shown, undefined, undefined, { context: MERGE_CONTEXT_LINES });
  |   const merged = applyPatch(real, patch, { fuzzFactor: 0 });

  | /** The board's edits of one file, taken out of the user's text (`rest`) and put back into a merged text. */
  | export interface BoardEdits {
  |   /** The user's text without those edits (the 3-line patch is applied to it). */
  |   rest: string;
  |   /** The merged text with the edits put back by card id, or undefined when they no longer fit. */
  |   restore(merged: string): string | undefined;
  | }
  | 
  | const blockText = (file: ParsedFile, block: MarkupBlock): string => file.text.slice(block.span.start, block.span.end);
  | const escapeRegExp = (s: string): string => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&');
  | 
  | /**
  |  * The edits the board makes while a run works (turn 13), when they are ALL the user changed in `real` apart from
  |  * text the 3-line patch can still place:
  |  *  - a card added by the A|D|G box: a new block (an id the base does not have, `id:` written, no `lean:` field,
  |  *    bound to nothing) followed by nothing but blank space and other such blocks up to the `end <namespace>` line
  |  *    of its (single) namespace, exactly where `add-node` puts it; it goes back to the end of that namespace;
  |  *  - a rationale written or changed on an existing card (the board asks for one on every assumption): the block
  |  *    reads exactly as in the base once its `rationale:` field is set back; the field goes back on that card. A
  |  *    rationale has no formal effect; every other field (the sentence above all) keeps the 3-line rule, so a
  |  *    sentence changed next to Claude's formalisation of it stays a conflict.
  |  * Undefined when there is no such edit, or when the file cannot be read as telelean text.
  |  */
  | export function boardEditsOf(path: string, base: string, real: string): BoardEdits | undefined {
  |   const b = parseLeanFile(path, base);
  |   const r = parseLeanFile(path, real);
  |   const baseBlocks = new Map(b.blocks.map((block) => [block.id, block]));
  |   if (baseBlocks.size !== b.blocks.length || new Set(r.blocks.map((block) => block.id)).size !== r.blocks.length) return undefined;
  | 
  |   // Rationales: set back to the base's value, after which the block must read exactly as in the base.
  |   const rationales: Array<{ id: string; value: string | null }> = [];
  |   const rationaleEdits = [];
  |   for (const block of r.blocks) {
  |     const was = baseBlocks.get(block.id);
  |     if (was === undefined || blockText(r, block) === blockText(b, was)) continue;
  |     if ((block.fields.rationale ?? null) === (was.fields.rationale ?? null)) continue;
  |     rationales.push({ id: block.id, value: block.fields.rationale ?? null });
  |     rationaleEdits.push(...setBlockField(r, block, 'rationale', was.fields.rationale ?? null));
  |   }
  |   let rest = rationaleEdits.length > 0 ? applyTextEdits(real, rationaleEdits) : real;
  |   if (rationales.length > 0) {
  |     const restFile = parseLeanFile(path, rest);
  |     for (const { id } of rationales) {
  |       const now = restFile.blocks.find((block) => block.id === id);
  |       const was = baseBlocks.get(id);
  |       if (now === undefined || was === undefined || blockText(restFile, now) !== blockText(b, was)) return undefined;
  |     }
  |   }
  | 
  |   // Added cards at the end of their namespace, in file order.
  |   const added = r.blocks.filter((block) => !baseBlocks.has(block.id));
  |   const cards: Array<{ id: string; text: string; namespace: string }> = [];
  |   for (let i = 0; i < added.length; i++) {
  |     const block = added[i] as MarkupBlock;
  |     const namespace = block.namespace;
  |     if (!block.idWritten || block.fields.lean !== undefined || block.binding.mode !== 'none' || namespace?.length !== 1) continue;
  |     const name = namespace[0] as string;
  |     const next = added[i + 1];
  |     const tail = real.slice(block.span.end, next !== undefined && cards.length >= 0 ? next.span.start : real.length);
  |     const atEnd = next !== undefined && /^\\s*$/.test(tail) ? true : new RegExp(`^\\\\s*end\\\\s+${escapeRegExp(name)}\\\\s*(?:\\\\n|$)`).test(real.slice(block.span.end));
  |     if (!atEnd) continue;
  |     cards.push({ id: block.id, text: blockText(r, block), namespace: name });
  |   }
  |   // Only a run of added blocks that reaches the namespace's end counts: drop any whose followers were not taken.
  |   while (cards.length > 0) {
  |     const lastCard = cards[cards.length - 1] as { id: string };
  |     const last = r.blocks.find((block) => block.id === lastCard.id) as MarkupBlock;
  |     if (new RegExp(`^\\\\s*end\\\\s+${escapeRegExp(cards[cards.length - 1]?.namespace ?? '')}\\\\b`).test(real.slice(last.span.end))) break;
  |     cards.pop();
  |   }
  |   for (const card of cards) {
  |     const file = parseLeanFile(path, rest);
  |     const block = file.blocks.find((x) => x.id === card.id);
  |     if (block === undefined) return undefined;
  |     rest = applyTextEdits(rest, removeBlockEdits(file, block));
  |   }
  |   if (rationales.length === 0 && cards.length === 0) return undefined;
  |

     /**
      * The edits the board makes while a run works (turn 13), when they are ALL the user changed in `real` apart from
      * text the 3-line patch can still place:
      *  - a card added by the A|D|G box: a new block (an id the base does not have, `id:` written, no `lean:` field,
      *    bound to nothing) followed by nothing but blank space and other such blocks up to the `end <namespace>` line
      *    of its (single) namespace, exactly where `add-node` puts it; it goes back to the end of that namespace;
      *  - a rationale written or changed on an existing card (the board asks for one on every assumption): the block
      *    reads exactly as in the base once its `rationale:` field is set back; the field goes back on that card. A
      *    rationale has no formal effect; every other field (the sentence above all) keeps the 3-line rule, so a
      *    sentence changed next to Claude's formalisation of it stays a conflict.

       private isTargetRef(run: ActiveRun, targets: readonly string[]): boolean {
         if (targets.length !== 1) return false;
         const t = targets[0] ?? '';
         const sel = run.p.selection;
         if (t === sel.target) return true;
         const statement = run.lastModel?.nodes.get(sel.target)?.node.lean.statement ?? this.graph(sel.subject)?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === sel.target)?.lean.statement;
         if (statement === undefined) return false;
         return t === statement || `${sel.subject}.${t}` === statement;
       }
